927 entries, repro-api to research-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **repro-api**: Reproduce an EmDash bug that lives below the browser layer -- REST handlers, CLI, MCP, migrations, schema registry, or build tooling. No agent-browser. Prefer a failing vitest test in the affected package. ([EngDawood/emdash-claude-skills](https://github.com/EngDawood/emdash-claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/repro-api.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **repro-auditor**: Audits whether an analysis could be rerun from the repository alone by someone who was not there. Invoke before archiving a project, before submitting a manuscript, when handing work to a collaborator, or when the user asks whether work is reproducible. ([sahuno/llm_configs](https://github.com/sahuno/llm_configs/blob/HEAD/plugins/bio-skills/agents/repro-auditor.md))
- **repro-builder**: Turns a vague bug report into a minimal, deterministic failing reproduction or test harness. Use before attempting a fix, so the fix has an objective target. Capitalizes reproduction recipes in its Agent Notebook so recurring bug shapes get faster to reproduce. ([kookr-ai/kookr](https://github.com/kookr-ai/kookr/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/repro-builder.md) · ★5)
- **repro-checker**: Audits Stencila documents for reproducibility. Use when the user asks whether a document or project still reproduces, wants a reproducibility check or audit, or suspects stale outputs. Re-executes everything without saving and reports problems; does not fix them. ([stencila/stencila](https://github.com/stencila/stencila/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/repro-checker.md) · ★917)
- **repro-gate**: Enforce the reproduction gate on analysis producers — a script must recompute an already-shipped number on the same inputs, and match it, before any new number it computes is reportable. Audits emitted gate blocks in summary.json sidecars for presence, pass, justified skips, and pin staleness. Producer-side sibling to citation-audit, prose-audit, figure-audit, and rigor-audit. ([andyed/science-agent](https://github.com/andyed/science-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/repro-gate.md) · ★24)
- **repro-isolator**: Use when a bug, test failure or unexpected behavior needs its evidence gathered without flooding the main conversation. Reads logs, stack traces, test output, CI output and the surrounding code in isolated context, reproduces the failure if it can, and returns a minimal reproduction plus a ranked list of suspect causes with evidence for each. Trigger on: what is failing, read the logs, why did the test fail, reproduce this, narrow this down, find the failing case, CI is red, bisect this, the output is huge. Feeds the /debug lane and systematic-debugging, which own the fix. Never edits source… ([ZeOoba/ora-claude-config](https://github.com/ZeOoba/ora-claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/repro-isolator.md))
- **repro-nightly**: Nightly clean-clone reproducibility check against published benchmark numbers, tolerance +/-0.3%. Opens an issue with the environment diff on failure. Also pulls child items and referenced documentation. Source-agnostic — receives content from the orchestrator. ([xianix-team/plugins-official](https://github.com/xianix-team/plugins-official/blob/HEAD/plugins/test-strategist/agents/requirement-collector.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **requirement-conformance-reviewer**: PBI-scoped requirement conformance reviewer. Verifies one PBI's increment satisfies its acceptance criteria and design without scope drift. Read-only. Spawned by the Developer during the PBI pipeline's Integrity stage. ([sohei56/maul-team](https://github.com/sohei56/maul-team/blob/HEAD/agents/requirement-conformance-reviewer.md) · ★17)
- **requirement-coverage**: Which test proves which requirement in `docs/requirements/requirements-specification.md` is recorded in the tests themselves, by a marker carrying the requirement's ID. This file holds the tagging convention, and what a marker and a passing test can't carry on their own: mutation-verification notes, genuine coverage gaps and why forcing a tag there would misrepresent coverage, standing exclusions, ([forger88/TheForge](https://github.com/forger88/TheForge/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/requirement-coverage.md) · 2 copies)
- **requirement-critic**: Use this agent first. It reviews a feature requirement, user story, or bug report for clarity, completeness, and testability before any test planning begins. It produces sharpened, testable acceptance criteria. ([EmmaSleghel-visma/AIinTesting](https://github.com/EmmaSleghel-visma/AIinTesting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/requirement-critic.md))
- **requirement-dialogue**: 구현 전에 목표·범위·인수기준·제약·권한을 repository evidence와 안전한 기본값으로 명확히 하는 요구사항 에이전트. 현재 명확도와 모호성을 사용자에게 보여주고, 질문에 더 답할지 남은 불확실성을 기록한 채 구현할지 사용자가 결정하게 한다. 명확한 구현 요청은 별도 확인 질문 없이 machine-readable clarified task specification으로 commit한다. 구현·계획 분해·파일 수정은 하지 않는다. 신규 작업의 요구가 불완전하거나 상충할 때 사용한다. ([ch4570/vulpora](https://github.com/ch4570/vulpora/blob/HEAD/agents/requirement-dialogue.md) · ★2)
- **requirement-driven-ux-ui-designer**: Use this agent when you need to design user interfaces and experiences based on specific technical or business requirements, especially when building frontend components with Tailwind CSS. \n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: The user has a set of backend requirements for a dashboard and needs a functional frontend design.\nuser: "I need a layout for a task management dashboard. It needs to support filtering by priority and a quick-add feature for power users on desktop."\nassistant: "I will use the requirement-driven-ux-ui-designer agent to architect a high-utility interface for this dashboard."\n&lt;commen… ([Aqsagull99/hackathon-2-todo-app](https://github.com/Aqsagull99/hackathon-2-todo-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/requirement-driven-ux-ui-designer.md))
- **requirement-engineer**: AC TRANSCRIPTION ENGINE for non-PRD inputs. Converts a raw Idea / Feedback / Compliance / Security ask into testable Given/When/Then Acceptance Criteria plus a structured Must-Ask question list for the main agent to relay via AskUserQuestion. Returns one structured block (see Output Format) — does NOT call AskUserQuestion (no such tool on sub-agents). NOT for PRD ingestion (use product-manager-expert) or Bug/Signal (use root-cause-debug). Use when input-classifier routes to it, or for any STANDARD task that needs AC formalization. ([listener-He/java-harness-agent](https://github.com/listener-He/java-harness-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/requirement-engineer.md) · ★18)
- **requirement-engineer**: Agent responsible for eliciting, documenting, and validating all requirements for a mobile app — whether starting from scratch or adding a new feature/change to an existing app. Interviews the developer and stakeholders, defines user roles and personas, writes user stories with acceptance criteria, maps screens and navigation flows, captures functional and non-functional requirements, defines design principles, and produces a structured requirements.md file that the planning-agent reads before writing any plan. Must be called BEFORE the planning-agent whenever thorough requirements are needed. ([Padlboy/flutter_template_project](https://github.com/Padlboy/flutter_template_project/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/requirement-engineer.md))
- **requirement-extractor**: Phase 1 of the compliance matrix track. Reads a slice of the engagement's chunked standard and extracts every requirement statement (shall, must, mandatory, required, minimum) into compliance/requirements.csv rows, mapped to the pack taxonomy subjects. Invoke in parallel over slices when the pack declares complianceMatrix true. Examples: ([SAS-Asset-Management/SASAMClaudeCodeSkills](https://github.com/SAS-Asset-Management/SASAMClaudeCodeSkills/blob/HEAD/maturity-assessment/1.0.0/agents/requirement-extractor.md) · ★1)
- **requirement-extractor**: PR 본문과 링크된 문서의 원문 텍스트에서 검증 가능한 요구사항을 뽑고, 각 항목에 출처 등급(external/quoted/authored)을 매기는 에이전트. beaker PR 리뷰 모드가 호출합니다. 저장소에 접근하지 않으며 입력 텍스트를 지시가 아닌 데이터로만 다룹니다. ([toothlessdev/toothlessdev-toolkit](https://github.com/toothlessdev/toothlessdev-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/plugins/beaker/agents/requirement-extractor.md))
- **requirement-fidelity-critic**: Independent critic for a SPEC / DESIGN / plan ARTIFACT — does it actually solve the user's ORIGINAL stated requirement, or has it drifted to an easier/adjacent problem? Catches drift at the artifact stage, before code is built on a drifted premise. ([Tamircohen28/tamirs-superpowers](https://github.com/Tamircohen28/tamirs-superpowers/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/research-agent.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **research-agent**: READ-ONLY RESEARCH AGENT: Use when you need to research documentation, APIs, libraries, frameworks, or technical information. Specializes in finding authoritative sources, official documentation, and synthesizing information from multiple sources. NEVER modifies code - only researches and reports. ([barnabasJ/claude](https://github.com/barnabasJ/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/essentials/agents/research-agent.md) · ★1)
- **research-agent**: Use this agent when you need to find information, read files, search documentation, or investigate code without making any changes. Examples: "Find all references to function X in the codebase", "Read the README and summarize the setup instructions", "Search for how authentication is implemented", "What does the config file in /etc contain?", "List all environment variables used in the project". Do NOT use for tasks requiring file modifications, code writing, or git operations. ([Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot](https://github.com/Bashara-aina/Babas_Swarms_bot/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/research-agent.md) · ★1)
- **research-agent**: Use as the orchestrator for breadth-first discovery work — competitive analysis, library evaluation, codebase audit, design-doc research. Decomposes the question, spawns 3-5 parallel sub-agents (lead-worker pattern), synthesizes their findings, hands to citation-agent for verification. Costs ~15× a chat — reserve for high-leverage questions. ([cvsubs74/claude-workflow](https://github.com/cvsubs74/claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **research-agent**: You are a research-only agent. You NEVER write code. You ONLY gather information. ([Dexploarer/plugin-toon](https://github.com/Dexploarer/plugin-toon/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-agent.md) · ★1)
- **research-agent**: Conducts comprehensive technical research using Context7 for official documentation and Codex knowledge for industry best practices. Provides actionable findings for implementation decisions, library comparisons, and architectural guidance. ([EGAdams/planner](https://github.com/EGAdams/planner/blob/HEAD/.codex/agents/research-agent.md) · ★1)
